<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="English">English</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: English"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:English_pronunciation" title="Appendix:English pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/meɪk/</span>, <span class="IPA nowrap">[meɪkʲ]</span></li>
<li><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content"><span class="usage-label-accent"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canadian_English" class="extiw" title="w:Canadian English">Canada</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multicultural_London_English" class="extiw" title="w:Multicultural London English">MLE</a></span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span> <a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:English_pronunciation" title="Appendix:English pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">[meːk]</span></li>
<li><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content"><span class="usage-label-accent"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Northumbrian_dialect" class="extiw" title="w:Northumbrian dialect">Northumbria</a></span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span> <a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:English_pronunciation" title="Appendix:English pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/mɛk/</span>
<ul><li><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content"><span class="usage-label-accent"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mackem" class="extiw" title="w:Mackem">Wearside</a></span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span> <a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:English_pronunciation" title="Appendix:English pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/mæk/</span></li></ul></li>
<li><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r50165410">.mw-parser-output .k-player .k-attribution{visibility:hidden}</style><table class="audiotable" style="vertical-align: middle; display: inline-block; list-style: none; line-height: 1em; border-collapse: collapse; margin: 0;"><tbody><tr><td>Audio <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="usage-label-accent"><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/British_English" class="extiw" title="w:British English">UK</a></span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span><span class="ib-semicolon qualifier-semicolon">;</span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><span class="ib-quote qualifier-quote">“</span>to make<span class="ib-quote qualifier-quote">”</span></i><span class="ib-colon qualifier-colon">:</span></td><td class="audiofile"><span typeof="mw:File"><span><audio id="mwe_player_0" controls="" preload="none" data-mw-tmh="" class="mw-file-element" width="175" style="width:175px;" data-durationhint="2" data-mwtitle="en-uk-to_make.ogg" data-mwprovider="wikimediacommons"><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/4/4d/En-uk-to_make.ogg" type="audio/ogg; codecs=&quot;vorbis&quot;" data-width="0" data-height="0" /><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/transcoded/4/4d/En-uk-to_make.ogg/En-uk-to_make.ogg.mp3" type="audio/mpeg" data-transcodekey="mp3" data-width="0" data-height="0" /></audio></span></span></td><td class="audiometa" style="font-size: 80%;">(<a href="/wiki/File:en-uk-to_make.ogg" title="File:en-uk-to make.ogg">file</a>)</td></tr></tbody></table></li>
<li><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r50165410" /><table class="audiotable" style="vertical-align: middle; display: inline-block; list-style: none; line-height: 1em; border-collapse: collapse; margin: 0;"><tbody><tr><td>Audio <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="usage-label-accent"><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/American_English" class="extiw" title="w:American English">US</a></span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span><span class="ib-colon qualifier-colon">:</span></td><td class="audiofile"><span typeof="mw:File"><span><audio id="mwe_player_1" controls="" preload="none" data-mw-tmh="" class="mw-file-element" width="175" style="width:175px;" data-durationhint="1" data-mwtitle="en-us-make.ogg" data-mwprovider="wikimediacommons"><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/0/03/En-us-make.ogg" type="audio/ogg; codecs=&quot;vorbis&quot;" data-width="0" data-height="0" /><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/transcoded/0/03/En-us-make.ogg/En-us-make.ogg.mp3" type="audio/mpeg" data-transcodekey="mp3" data-width="0" data-height="0" /></audio></span></span></td><td class="audiometa" style="font-size: 80%;">(<a href="/wiki/File:en-us-make.ogg" title="File:en-us-make.ogg">file</a>)</td></tr></tbody></table></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_1">Etymology 1</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Etymology 1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul class="etymid" id="English:&#95;make" data-lang="en" data-id="make"></ul>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_English" class="extiw" title="w:Middle English">Middle English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/maken#Middle_English" title="maken">maken</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_English" class="extiw" title="w:Old English">Old English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ang"><a href="/wiki/macian#Old_English" title="macian">macian</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to make, build, work</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-West_Germanic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-West Germanic language">Proto-West Germanic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gmw-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-West_Germanic/mak%C5%8Dn" title="Reconstruction:Proto-West Germanic/makōn">&#42;makōn</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to make, build, work</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/meh%E2%82%82%C7%B5-&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/meh₂ǵ- (page does not exist)">&#42;meh₂ǵ-</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to knead, mix, make</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p><p>Related to <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/match#English" title="match">match</a></i>.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Verb">Verb</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Verb"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="en">make</strong> (<i>third-person singular simple present</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en s-verb-form-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/makes#English" title="makes">makes</a></b>, <i>present participle</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en ing-form-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/making#English" title="making">making</a></b>, <i>simple past and past participle</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en ed-form-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/made#English" title="made">made</a></b> <i>or</i> <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content"><span class="usage-label-sense">dialectal or obsolete</span></span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en ed-form-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/maked#English" title="maked">maked</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/create" title="create">create</a>.
<ol><li>To <a href="/wiki/build" title="build">build</a>, <a href="/wiki/construct" title="construct">construct</a>, <a href="/wiki/produce" title="produce">produce</a>, or <a href="/wiki/originate" title="originate">originate</a>.
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/fabricate#English" title="fabricate">fabricate</a></span>; <i>see also</i> <a href="/wiki/Thesaurus:build#English" title="Thesaurus:build">Thesaurus:<span class="Latn" lang="en">build</span></a></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">We <b>made</b> a bird feeder for our yard.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">I'll <b>make</b> a man out of him yet.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He <b>makes</b> deodorants.</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1892</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walter_Besant" class="extiw" title="w:Walter Besant">Walter Besant</a>, “Prologue: Who is Edmund Gray?”, in <cite>The Ivory Gate<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="A Novel">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harper_(publisher)" class="extiw" title="w:Harper (publisher)">Harper &amp; Brothers</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Franklin Square">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/16832619">→OCLC</a></small>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Thus, when he drew up instructions in lawyer language, he expressed the important words by an initial, a medial, or a final consonant, and <b>made</b> scratches for all the words between&#59; his clerks, however, understood him very well.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1913</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joseph_C._Lincoln" class="extiw" title="w:Joseph C. Lincoln">Joseph C&#91;rosby&#93; Lincoln</a>, chapter VII, in <cite>Mr. Pratt’s Patients</cite>, New York, N.Y.&#59; London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/D._Appleton_%26_Company" class="extiw" title="w:D. Appleton &amp; Company">D&#91;aniel&#93; Appleton and Company</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1650302">→OCLC</a></small>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">I <b>made</b> a speaking trumpet of my hands and commenced to whoop “Ahoy&#33;” and “Hello&#33;” at the top of my lungs. <span class="q-hellip-sp"> </span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#91;</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">&#93;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"> </span> The Colonel woke up, and, after asking what in brimstone was the matter, opened his mouth and roared “Hi&#33;” and “Hello&#33;” like the bull of Bashan.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>2013</b> June 22, “T time”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Economist" class="extiw" title="w:The Economist">The Economist</a></cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://archive.ph/JN1ea">[1]</a></sup>, volume <span class="None" lang="und">407</span>, number <span class="None" lang="und">8841</span>, archived from <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="http://www.economist.com/news/finance-and-economics/21579860-g8-pledges-tackle-three-ts-t-time">the original</a> on <span class="mw-formatted-date" title="2023-03-11">11 March 2023</span>, page <span class="None" lang="und">68</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Yet in “Through a Latte, Darkly”, a new study of how Starbucks has largely avoided paying tax in Britain, Edward Kleinbard<span class="q-hellip-sp"> </span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#91;</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">&#93;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"> </span>shows that current tax rules make it easy for all sorts of firms to generate what he calls “stateless income”&#58;<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#91;</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">&#93;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>. In Starbucks’s case, the firm has in effect turned the process of <b>making</b> an expensive cup of coffee into intellectual property.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li>To <a href="/wiki/write" title="write">write</a> or <a href="/wiki/compose" title="compose">compose</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">I <b>made</b> a poem for her wedding.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He <b>made</b> a will.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li>To <a href="/wiki/bring_about" title="bring about">bring about</a>; to <a href="/wiki/effect" title="effect">effect</a> or <a href="/wiki/produce" title="produce">produce</a> by means of some action.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><b>make</b> war</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">They were just a bunch of ne'er-do-wells who went around <b>making</b> trouble for honest men.</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>2021</b> February 20, Hannah Selinger, “Who Is Still Buying VHS Tapes?”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_New_York_Times" class="extiw" title="w:The New York Times">The New York Times</a></cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://web.archive.org/web/20220328033549/https://www.nytimes.com/2021/02/20/style/vhs-tapes.html">[2]</a></sup>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://www.worldcat.org/issn/0362-4331">→ISSN</a></small>, archived from <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://www.nytimes.com/2021/02/20/style/vhs-tapes.html">the original</a> on <span class="mw-formatted-date" title="2022-03-28">28 March 2022</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">But whether or not the VCR <b>makes</b> a complete comeback, VHS enthusiasts agree that these tapes occupy an irreplaceable place in culture.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">religious</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/create" title="create">create</a> (the universe), especially <span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">in Christianity</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> from <a href="/wiki/nothing" title="nothing">nothing</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">God <b>made</b> earth and heaven.</i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1850</b></span>, &#91;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alfred,_Lord_Tennyson" class="extiw" title="w:Alfred, Lord Tennyson">Alfred, Lord Tennyson</a>&#93;, “Prologue”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/In_Memoriam_A.H.H." class="extiw" title="w:In Memoriam A.H.H.">In Memoriam</a></cite>, London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edward_Moxon" class="extiw" title="w:Edward Moxon">Edward Moxon</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Dover Street">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/3968433">→OCLC</a></small>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Thine are these orbs of light and shade&#59;<br />⁠Thou <b>madest</b> Life in man and brute&#59;<br />⁠Thou <b>madest</b> Death&#59; and lo, thy foot<br />Is on the skull which thou hast <b>made</b>.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/prepare" title="prepare">prepare</a> (food); to <a href="/wiki/cook" title="cook">cook</a> (food).
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">I'm <b>making</b> cereal for breakfast. Who wants some&#63;</i></div></dd></dl></li></ol></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;now mostly&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#colloquial" title="Appendix:Glossary">colloquial</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/behave" title="behave">behave</a>, to <a href="/wiki/act" title="act">act</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">To <b>make</b> like a deer caught in the headlights.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">They <b>made</b> nice together, as if their fight never happened.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">He <b>made</b> as if to punch him, but they both laughed and shook hands.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To tend; to contribute; to have effect; with <i>for</i> or <i>against</i>.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1873</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Matthew_Arnold" class="extiw" title="w:Matthew Arnold">Matthew Arnold</a>, <cite><a href="https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/Literature_and_Dogma" class="extiw" title="s:Literature and Dogma">Literature and Dogma</a></cite>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">And all Israel's language about this power, except that it <b>makes</b> for righteousness, is approximate language</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1611</b></span>, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bible" class="extiw" title="w:Bible">The Holy Bible,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Conteyning the Old Testament, and the New. Newly Translated out of the Originall Tongues: &amp; with the Former Translations Diligently Compared and Reuised, by His Maiesties Speciall Comandement. Appointed to be Read in Churches.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></a></cite> (<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/King_James_Version" class="extiw" title="w:King James Version">King James Version</a>), London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Imprinted at London by">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Barker_(printer)" class="extiw" title="w:Robert Barker (printer)">Robert Barker</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="printer to the Kings Most Excellent Maiestie.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/964384981">→OCLC</a></small>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Epistle_to_the_Romans" class="extiw" title="w:Epistle to the Romans">Romans</a> <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://www.kingjamesbibleonline.org/1611_Romans-Chapter-14/#19">14&#58;19</a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Follow after the things which <b>make</b> for peace.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#c." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="circa">c.</abbr></a></i> <span class="None" lang="und"><b>1597</b></span> (date written), <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Shakespeare" class="extiw" title="w:William Shakespeare">William Shakespeare</a>, “<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Henry_IV,_Part_1" class="extiw" title="w:Henry IV, Part 1">The First Part of Henry the Fourth,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="with the Life and Death of Henry Sirnamed Hot-spvrre.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></a>”, in <cite>Mr. William Shakespeares Comedies, Histories, &amp; Tragedies<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Published According to the True Originall Copies.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite> (<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/First_Folio" class="extiw" title="w:First Folio">First Folio</a>), London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed by">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Jaggard" class="extiw" title="w:William Jaggard">Isaac Iaggard</a>, and <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edward_Blount" class="extiw" title="w:Edward Blount">Ed<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>ward<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Blount</a>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1623</span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/606515358">→OCLC</a></small>, <span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>Act V, scene i<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Considerations infinite <br /> Do <b>make</b> against it.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li>To <a href="/wiki/constitute" title="constitute">constitute</a>.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">They <b>make</b> a cute couple.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">This <b>makes</b> the third infraction.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/one_swallow_does_not_a_summer_make#English" title="one swallow does not a summer make">One swallow does not a summer <b>make</b>.</a></i></div></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>1897</b> December (indicated as <b>1898</b>), <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Winston_Churchill_(novelist)" class="extiw" title="w:Winston Churchill (novelist)">Winston Churchill</a>, chapter V, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Celebrity" class="extiw" title="w:The Celebrity">The Celebrity&#58; An Episode</a></cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Macmillan_Inc." class="extiw" title="w:Macmillan Inc.">The Macmillan Company</a>; London&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Macmillan_Publishers" class="extiw" title="w:Macmillan Publishers">Macmillan &amp; Co., Ltd.</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/222716698">→OCLC</a></small>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">We <b>made</b> an odd party before the arrival of the Ten, particularly when the Celebrity dropped in for lunch or dinner. He could not be induced to remain permanently at Mohair because Miss Trevor was at Asquith, but he appropriated a Hempstead cart from the Mohair stables and made the trip sometimes twice in a day.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>

<dl><dd>Style alone does not <b>make</b> a writer.</dd></dl></li>

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/add_up_to" title="add up to">add up to</a>, have a <a href="/wiki/sum" title="sum">sum</a> of.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Two and four <b>make</b> six.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;construed with <i>of</i><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;typically interrogative</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/interpret" title="interpret">interpret</a>.

<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">What time do you <b>make</b> it&#63;</i></div></dd></dl></li>

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#ditransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">ditransitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;second object is an adjective or participle</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/cause" title="cause">cause</a> to be.

<li>To cause to appear to be; to represent as.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Homer <b>makes</b> Aphrodite the daughter of Zeus, unlike Hesiod who depicted her as born from the sea foam.</i></div></dd></dl>

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#ditransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">ditransitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;second object is a verb</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/cause" title="cause">cause</a> (to do something); to <a href="/wiki/compel" title="compel">compel</a> (to do something).

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1892</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Walter_Besant" class="extiw" title="w:Walter Besant">Walter Besant</a>, chapter III, in <cite>The Ivory Gate<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="A Novel">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, New York, N.Y.&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harper_(publisher)" class="extiw" title="w:Harper (publisher)">Harper &amp; Brothers</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Franklin Square">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/16832619">→OCLC</a></small>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">In former days every tavern of repute kept such a room for its own select circle, a club, or society, of habitués, who met every evening, for a pipe and a cheerful glass.<span class="q-hellip-sp"> </span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#91;</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">&#93;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"> </span>Strangers might enter the room, but they were <b>made</b> to feel that they were there on sufferance&#58; they were received with distance and suspicion.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#colloquial" title="Appendix:Glossary">colloquial</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To <a href="/wiki/arrive" title="arrive">arrive</a> at a <a href="/wiki/destination" title="destination">destination</a>, usually at or by a certain time.
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">We should <b>make</b> Cincinnati by 7 tonight.</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">I won't be able to <b>make</b> dinner&#59; something's come up at work.</i></div></dd></dl>

<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#colloquial" title="Appendix:Glossary">colloquial</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To proceed (in a direction).

<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#c." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="circa">c.</abbr></a></i> <b>1597</b> (date written), <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Shakespeare" class="extiw" title="w:William Shakespeare">William Shakespeare</a>, “<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Merry_Wives_of_Windsor" class="extiw" title="w:The Merry Wives of Windsor">The Merry Wiues of Windsor</a>”, in <cite>Mr. William Shakespeares Comedies, Histories, &amp; Tragedies<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Published According to the True Originall Copies.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite> (<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/First_Folio" class="extiw" title="w:First Folio">First Folio</a>), London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed by">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Jaggard" class="extiw" title="w:William Jaggard">Isaac Iaggard</a>, and <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edward_Blount" class="extiw" title="w:Edward Blount">Ed<span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>ward<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> Blount</a>, published <span class="None" lang="und">1623</span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/606515358">→OCLC</a></small>, <span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>Act I, scene iv<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">a scurvy, jack-a-nape priest to meddle or <b>make</b></span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#obsolete" title="Appendix:Glossary">obsolete</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To increase; to augment; to accrue.</li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#obsolete" title="Appendix:Glossary">obsolete</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To be engaged or concerned in.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1681</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Dryden" class="extiw" title="w:John Dryden">John Dryden</a>, <cite>The Spanish Fryar: Or, the Double Discovery.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Acted at the Duke’s Theatre.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></cite>, London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed for">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> Richard Tonson and <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jacob_Tonson" class="extiw" title="w:Jacob Tonson">Jacob Tonson</a>,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="at Grays-inn-gate, in Grays-inn-lane, and at the Judge’s-Head, in Chancery-lane.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/6484883">→OCLC</a></small>, <span class="maintenance-line">(please specify the page number)</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Gomez, what <b>makest</b> thou here, with a whole brotherhood of city bailiffs&#63;</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">now&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#archaic" title="Appendix:Glossary">archaic</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To cause to <a href="/wiki/be" title="be">be</a> (in a specified place), used after a subjective <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/what#English" title="what">what</a></i>.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1676</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Etherege" class="extiw" title="w:George Etherege">George Etherege</a>, <cite>The Man of Mode</cite>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en"><i>Footman.</i> Madam&#33; Mr. Dorimant&#33;<br /><i>Lov.</i> What <b>makes</b> him here&#63;</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1816</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samuel_Taylor_Coleridge" class="extiw" title="w:Samuel Taylor Coleridge">Samuel Taylor Coleridge</a>, <cite>Christabel</cite>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">What makes <b>her</b> in the wood so late, <br /> A furlong from the castle gate&#63;</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#euphemism" title="Appendix:Glossary">euphemistic</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To take the <a href="/wiki/virginity" title="virginity">virginity</a> of.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1896</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rudyard_Kipling" class="extiw" title="w:Rudyard Kipling">Rudyard Kipling</a>, <cite><a href="https://en.wikisource.org/wiki/The_Seven_Seas/The_Ladies" class="extiw" title="s:The Seven Seas/The Ladies">The Ladies</a></cite>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">I was a young un at 'Oogli,<br />Shy as a girl to begin&#59;<br />Aggie de Castrer she <b>made</b> me,<br />— An' Aggie was clever as sin&#59;<br />Older than me, but my first un —<br />More like a mother she were<br />Showed me the way to promotion an' pay,<br />An' I learned about women from 'er&#33;</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To have <a href="/wiki/sexual_intercourse" title="sexual intercourse">sexual intercourse</a> with.
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <i>see</i> <a href="/wiki/Thesaurus:copulate_with#English" title="Thesaurus:copulate with">Thesaurus:<span class="Latn" lang="en">copulate with</span></a></span></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1934</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_T._Farrell" class="extiw" title="w:James T. Farrell">James T. Farrell</a>, chapter 16, in <cite>The Young Manhood of Studs Lonigan</cite>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">He could see that her face was thin, proud. She looked like she'd be a hard dame to <b>make</b>. He didn't want just that. She'd be a hard dame to win.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1959</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vance_Packard" class="extiw" title="w:Vance Packard">Vance Packard</a>, <cite>The Status Seekers</cite>, Pocket Books, published <span class="None" lang="und">1971</span>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/978-0-671-77115-7" title="Special:BookSources/978-0-671-77115-7">→ISBN</a></small>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/statusseekers1959pack/page/138/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">138</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">The boys in the lower classes who had already dropped out of school derived much of their prestige among their peers from their skill in “<b>making</b>” girls.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1979</b></span>, Mark Tuttle, “The Loan Shark”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Three%27s_Company" class="extiw" title="w:Three&#39;s Company">Three's Company</a></cite>, season 4, episode <span class="None" lang="und">10</span> (television production):</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">The only thing she wants to <b>make</b> is you&#33;</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1996</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rivers_Cuomo" class="extiw" title="w:Rivers Cuomo">Rivers Cuomo</a>, “Tired of Sex”, in <cite>Pinkerton</cite>, performed by Weezer:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Monday night, I'm <b>makin'</b> Jen <br /> Tuesday night, I'm <b>makin'</b> Lyn <br /> Wednesday night, I'm <b>makin'</b> Catherine <br /> Oh, why can't I be makin' love come true&#63;</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#intransitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">intransitive</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> Of water, to <a href="/wiki/flow" title="flow">flow</a> toward land; to <a href="/wiki/rise" title="rise">rise</a>.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1899</b></span> February, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joseph_Conrad" class="extiw" title="w:Joseph Conrad">Joseph Conrad</a>, “<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Heart_of_Darkness" class="extiw" title="w:Heart of Darkness">The Heart of Darkness</a>”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blackwood%27s_Magazine" class="extiw" title="w:Blackwood&#39;s Magazine">Blackwood’s Edinburgh Magazine</a></cite>, volume CLXV, number M, New York, N.Y.&#58; The Leonard Scott Publishing Company,<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="7 and 9 Warren Street">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/1042815524">→OCLC</a></small>, part I, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://archive.org/details/1899heartofdarknessconradfebmaraprilblackwoodsmagazine/page/n1/mode/1up">page <span class="None" lang="und">193</span></a>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">The flood had <b>made</b>, the wind was nearly calm, and being bound down the river, the only thing for us was to come to and wait for the turn of the tide.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#transitive" title="Appendix:Glossary">transitive</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/backgammon" title="backgammon">backgammon</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> To establish two or more men on (a <a href="/wiki/point" title="point">point</a>) so that it cannot be <a href="/wiki/capture" title="capture">captured</a>.</li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading5"><h5 id="Usage_notes">Usage notes</h5><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Usage notes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li>In older forms of English, when the pronoun <i><a href="/wiki/thou" title="thou">thou</a></i> was in active use, and verbs used <i><a href="/wiki/-est" title="-est">-est</a></i> for distinct second-person singular indicative forms, the verb <i>make</i> had the form <i><a href="/wiki/makest" title="makest">makest</a></i>, and had <i><a href="/wiki/madest" title="madest">madest</a></i> for its past tense.</li>
<li>Similarly, when the ending <i><a href="/wiki/-eth" title="-eth">-eth</a></i> was in active use for third-person singular present indicative forms, the form <i><a href="/wiki/maketh" title="maketh">maketh</a></i> was used.</li></ul>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Noun">Noun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="en">make</strong> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en p-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/makes#English" title="makes">makes</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li class="mw-empty-elt"></li><li class="senseid" id="English:&#95;brand,&#95;marque,&#95;manufacturer" data-lang="en" data-id="brand, marque, manufacturer"> <a href="/wiki/brand#Noun" title="brand">Brand</a>; <a href="/wiki/marque#English:_brand,_marque,_manufacturer" title="marque">marque</a>; <a href="/wiki/manufacturer" title="manufacturer">manufacturer</a>; <a href="/wiki/maker" title="maker">maker</a>.
<dl><dd><span class="nyms coordinate-term"><span style="font-size: smaller">Coordinate term:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/model#English:_style" title="model">model</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="nyms near-synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Near-synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/kind#Noun" title="kind">kind</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/type#Noun" title="type">type</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example collocation"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">we service all <b>makes</b> and models</i></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="en">Q&#58; What <b>make</b> and model of car do you drive&#63; A&#58; It's a Buick LaCrosse.</i></div></dd></dl></li>
<li class="mw-empty-elt"></li><li class="senseid" id="English:&#95;manner&#95;or&#95;style&#95;of&#95;construction" data-lang="en" data-id="manner or style of construction"> <a href="/wiki/manner" title="manner">Manner</a> or <a href="/wiki/style" title="style">style</a> of <a href="/wiki/construction" title="construction">construction</a> (style of how a thing is made).
<dl><dd><span class="nyms near-synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Near-synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/makeup#Noun" title="makeup">makeup</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/form#Noun" title="form">form</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/conformation#English" title="conformation">conformation</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/composition#English" title="composition">composition</a></span>; <span class="Latn" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/form_factor#English" title="form factor">form factor</a></span></span></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1907</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mark_Twain" class="extiw" title="w:Mark Twain">Mark Twain</a>, <cite>A Horse's Tale</cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="http://www.gutenberg.org/dirs/etext97/hrstl10.txt">[4]</a></sup>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">I can name the tribe every moccasin belongs to by the <b>make</b> of it.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_2">Etymology 2</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Etymology 2"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul class="etymid" id="English:&#95;mate" data-lang="en" data-id="mate"></ul>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_English" class="extiw" title="w:Middle English">Middle English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="enm"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Middle_English:_equal">make</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/imake#Middle_English" title="imake">imake</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/%C8%9Demace#Middle_English" title="ȝemace">ȝemace</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_English" class="extiw" title="w:Old English">Old English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ang"><a href="/wiki/gemaca#Old_English" title="gemaca">ġemaca</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">a mate, an equal, companion, peer</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-West_Germanic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-West Germanic language">Proto-West Germanic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gmw-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-West_Germanic/gamak%C5%8D&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-West Germanic/gamakō (page does not exist)">&#42;gamakō</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Germanic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Germanic language">Proto-Germanic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gem-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/gamak%C3%B4&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/gamakô (page does not exist)">&#42;gamakô</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">companion, comrade</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Indo-European_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Indo-European language">Proto-Indo-European</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ine-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/ma%C7%B5-&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Indo-European/maǵ- (page does not exist)">&#42;maǵ-</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">to knead, oil</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>. Reinforced by <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Norse" class="extiw" title="w:Old Norse">Old Norse</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="non"><a href="/wiki/maki#Old_Norse" title="maki">maki</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">an equal</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p><p>Cognate with <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Icelandic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Icelandic language">Icelandic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="is"><a href="/wiki/maki#Icelandic" title="maki">maki</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">spouse</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Swedish_language" class="extiw" title="w:Swedish language">Swedish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="sv"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Swedish">make</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">spouse, husband</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Danish_language" class="extiw" title="w:Danish language">Danish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="da"><a href="/wiki/mage#Danish" title="mage">mage</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">companion, fellow, mate</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>. <a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#doublet" title="Appendix:Glossary">Doublet</a> of <i class="Latn mention" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/match#English" title="match">match</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Noun_2">Noun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="en">make</strong> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en p-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/makes#English" title="makes">makes</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#slang" title="Appendix:Glossary">slang</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;usually in phrase "easy make"</span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> Past, present<span class="serial-comma">,</span> or future target of <a href="/wiki/seduction" title="seduction">seduction</a> (usually female).
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>2007</b></span>, Prudence Mors Rains, <cite>Becoming an Unwed Mother</cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="http://books.google.com/books?id=Lo8VtTjhG-gC">[14]</a></sup>, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/020230955X" title="Special:BookSources/020230955X">→ISBN</a></small>, page <span class="None" lang="und">26</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">To me, if I weren't going with someone and was taking pills, it would be like advertising that I'm an easy <b>make</b>.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1962</b></span>, Ralph Moreno, <cite>A Man's Estate</cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="http://books.google.com/books?id=l7AnAAAAMAAJ">[15]</a></sup>, page <span class="None" lang="und">12</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">She's your <b>make</b>, not mine. <span class="q-hellip-sp"> </span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#91;</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">&#93;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"> </span> It isn't anything short of difficult to entertain someone else's pregnant fiancee.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/British_English" class="extiw" title="w:British English">UK</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#dialectal" title="Appendix:Glossary">dialectal</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/mate" title="mate">Mate</a>; a <a href="/wiki/spouse" title="spouse">spouse</a> or <a href="/wiki/companion" title="companion">companion</a>; a <a href="/wiki/match" title="match">match</a>.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1590</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edmund_Spenser" class="extiw" title="w:Edmund Spenser">Edmund Spenser</a>, “Book I, Canto VII”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Faerie_Queene" class="extiw" title="w:The Faerie Queene">The Faerie Queene.<span class="q-hellip-sp">&#160;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Disposed into Twelue Bookes, Fashioning XII. Morall Vertues.">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b"></span></a></cite>, London&#58; <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span></span><span title="Printed &#91;by&#93;">…</span><span class="q-hellip-b"><span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> &#91;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Wolfe_(printer)" class="extiw" title="w:John Wolfe (printer)">John Wolfe</a>&#93; for <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Ponsonby_(publisher)" class="extiw" title="w:William Ponsonby (publisher)">William Ponsonbie</a>, <small><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://search.worldcat.org/title/960102938">→OCLC</a></small>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Th'Elfe therewith astownd, <br /> Vpstarted lightly from his looser <b>make</b>, <br /> And his vnready weapons gan in hand to take.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1624</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ben_Jonson" class="extiw" title="w:Ben Jonson">Ben Jonson</a>, <cite>The Masque of Owls at Kenilworth</cite>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">Where their maids and their <b>makes</b> <br /> At dancing and wakes, <br /> Had their napkins and posies <br /> And the wipers for their noses</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1684</b></span>, Meriton, <cite>Praise Ale</cite>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">But then sometimes I thought, it's a black Crake <br /> That never to her-sell can get a <b>Make</b>.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><b>1678</b> (later reprinted: <b>1855</b>), John Ray, <i>A Hand-book of Proverbs</i>:
<dl><dd>Every cake hath its <b>make</b>; but a scrape cake hath two.</dd></dl></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Noun_3">Noun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scottish_English" class="extiw" title="w:Scottish English">Scotland</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hiberno-English" class="extiw" title="w:Hiberno-English">Ireland</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/English_language_in_Northern_England" class="extiw" title="w:English language in Northern England">Northern England</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;now&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#rare" title="Appendix:Glossary">rare</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> A <a href="/wiki/halfpenny" title="halfpenny">halfpenny</a>. <link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r86669767" /><span class="defdate">[from 16th c.]</span>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1826</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sir_Walter_Scott" class="extiw" title="w:Sir Walter Scott">Sir Walter Scott</a>, <cite>Woodstock; Or, the Cavalier</cite>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en">the last we shall have, I take it&#59; for a <b>make</b> to a million, but we trine to the nubbing cheat to-morrow.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><b>1934</b>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lewis_Grassic_Gibbon" class="extiw" title="w:Lewis Grassic Gibbon">Lewis Grassic Gibbon</a>, <i>Grey Granite</i>, Polygon 2006 <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content">A Scots Quair</span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span>, page 606:
<dl><dd>Only as he climbed the steps did he mind that he hadn't even a <b>meck</b> upon him, and turned to jump off as the tram with a showd swung grinding down to the Harbour <span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b">[</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">]</span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span></dd></dl></li></ul></li></ol>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_4">Etymology 4</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Etymology 4"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Origin unclear.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Noun_4">Noun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=21" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="en">make</strong> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-en p-form-of" lang="en"><a href="/wiki/makes#English" title="makes">makes</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/East_Anglian_English" class="extiw" title="w:East Anglian English">East Anglia</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Essex_dialect" class="extiw" title="w:Essex dialect">Essex</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#obsolete" title="Appendix:Glossary">obsolete</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> An agricultural tool resembling a <a href="/wiki/scythe" title="scythe">scythe</a>, used to cut (harvest) certain plants such as peas, reeds, or tares.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1797</b></span>, Arthur Young, <cite>General View of the Agriculture of the County of Suffolk: Drawn Up for the Consideration of the Board of Agriculture and Internal Improvement</cite>, page <span class="None" lang="und">73</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="en"><i>Harvest.</i>—When left for seed, they are cut and wadded as pease, with a <i><b>make</b></i>.<br /><i>Produce.</i>—From three to six sacks an acre.</span></div></dd></dl></div></li>
<li><b>1811</b>, William Gooch, <i>General view of the agriculture of the county of Cambridge; drawn up for the consideration of the Board of Agriculture and Internal Improvement</i>, page 142, section VI "Pease":
<dl><dd>Harvest. Taken up by a pease-<b>make</b>, and left in small heaps, and turned as often as the weather may make it necessary.</dd></dl></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Hawaiian">Hawaiian</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=28" title="Edit section: Hawaiian"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=29" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Polynesian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Polynesian language">Proto-Polynesian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="poz-pol-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Polynesian/mate" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Polynesian/mate">&#42;mate</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Malayo-Polynesian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian language">Proto-Malayo-Polynesian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="poz-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian/m-atay&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian/m-atay (page does not exist)">&#42;m-atay</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="poz-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian/atay&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Malayo-Polynesian/atay (page does not exist)">&#42;atay</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Austronesian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Austronesian language">Proto-Austronesian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="map-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Austronesian/m-aCay&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Austronesian/m-aCay (page does not exist)">&#42;m-aCay</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="map-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-Austronesian/aCay&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Austronesian/aCay (page does not exist)">&#42;aCay</a></i> (compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cebuano_language" class="extiw" title="w:Cebuano language">Cebuano</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ceb"><a href="/wiki/matay#Cebuano" title="matay">matay</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chamorro_language" class="extiw" title="w:Chamorro language">Chamorro</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ch"><a href="/wiki/matai#Chamorro" title="matai">matai</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fijian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Fijian language">Fijian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="fj"><a href="/wiki/mate#Fijian" title="mate">mate</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ilocano_language" class="extiw" title="w:Ilocano language">Ilocano</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ilo"><a href="/wiki/matay#Ilocano" title="matay">matay</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Indonesian language">Indonesian</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="id"><a href="/wiki/mati#Indonesian" title="mati">mati</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javanese_language" class="extiw" title="w:Javanese language">Javanese</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="jv"><a href="/wiki/mati#Javanese" title="mati">mati</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kapampangan_language" class="extiw" title="w:Kapampangan language">Kapampangan</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="pam"><a href="/wiki/mate#Kapampangan" title="mate">mate</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="pam"><a href="/wiki/mete#Kapampangan" title="mete">mete</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malagasy_language" class="extiw" title="w:Malagasy language">Malagasy</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="mg"><a href="/wiki/maty#Malagasy" title="maty">maty</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/M%C4%81ori_language" class="extiw" title="w:Māori <div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Loloda">Loloda</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=35" title="Edit section: Loloda"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_5">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=36" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Likely only reconstructible to Proto-Galela-Tobelo (Proto-Northeast Halmaheran), as <i>*make</i>; compare <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Galela_language" class="extiw" title="w:Galela language">Galela</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gbi"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Galela">make</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tabaru_language" class="extiw" title="w:Tabaru language">Tabaru</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="tby"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Tabaru">make</a></i>, <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tobelo_language" class="extiw" title="w:Tobelo language">Tobelo</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="tlb"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Tobelo">make</a></i>.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_1_2">Etymology 1</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=41" title="Edit section: Etymology 1"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul class="etymid" id="Middle&#95;English:&#95;equal" data-lang="enm" data-id="equal"></ul>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_English" class="extiw" title="w:Old English">Old English</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="ang"><a href="/wiki/maca#Old_English" title="maca">maca</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="ang"><a href="/wiki/gemaca#Old_English" title="gemaca">ġemaca</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-West_Germanic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-West Germanic language">Proto-West Germanic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gmw-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-West_Germanic/mak%C5%8D&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-West Germanic/makō (page does not exist)">&#42;makō</a></i>, <i class="Latn mention" lang="gmw-pro"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Reconstruction:Proto-West_Germanic/gamak%C5%8D&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Reconstruction:Proto-West Germanic/gamakō (page does not exist)">&#42;gamakō</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Germanic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Germanic language">Proto-Germanic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gem-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/mak%C3%B4" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/makô">&#42;makô</a></i>. <a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#doublet" title="Appendix:Glossary">Doublet</a> of <i class="Latn mention" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/macche#Middle_English" title="macche">macche</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">“</span><span class="mention-gloss">bride, equal</span><span class="mention-gloss-double-quote">”</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Alternative_forms_3">Alternative forms</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=42" title="Edit section: Alternative forms"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><span class="Latn" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/mac#Middle_English" title="mac">mac</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/mak#Middle_English" title="mak">mak</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/ymake#Middle_English" title="ymake">ymake</a></span></li>
<li><span class="Latn" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/%C8%9Demace#Middle_English" title="ȝemace">ȝemace</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/imake#Middle_English" title="imake">imake</a></span> <span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_English#Early_Middle_English" class="extiw" title="w:Middle English">Early Middle English</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Pronunciation_5">Pronunciation</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=43" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Middle_English_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Middle English pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈmaːk(ə)/</span>, <span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈmak(ə)/</span>, <span class="IPA nowrap">/iˈmaːk(ə)/</span></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Noun_6">Noun</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=44" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="enm">make</strong></span>&#32;(<i>plural</i> <b><span class="Latn form-of lang-enm pl-form-of" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/makes#Middle_English" title="makes">makes</a></span></b>&#32;<i>or</i> <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content">early</span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span>&#32;<b><span class="Latn form-of lang-enm pl-form-of" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/imaken#Middle_English" title="imaken">imaken</a></span></b>)
</p>
<ol><li>A <a href="/wiki/bride" title="bride">bride</a> or <a href="/wiki/mate" title="mate">mate</a>; a <a href="/wiki/romantic" title="romantic">romantic</a> <a href="/wiki/partner" title="partner">partner</a>.
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#c." title="Appendix:Glossary"><abbr title="circa">c.</abbr></a></i> <span class="None" lang="und"><b>1180</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Orrm" class="extiw" title="w:Orrm">Orͬm</a>, “Secundum Lucam .<span style="font-variant:small-caps;">I</span>. <span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>Luke 1:5<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span> / Secundum Lucam .<span style="font-variant:small-caps;">II</span>. <span style="font-style: normal;">&#91;</span>Luke 1:18<span style="font-style: normal;">&#93;</span>”, in <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ormulum" class="extiw" title="w:Ormulum">Orͬmulum</a></cite> (Bodleian MS. Junius 1), <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bourne_Abbey" class="extiw" title="w:Bourne Abbey">Bourne Abbey, Lincolnshire</a>, <a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://digital.bodleian.ox.ac.uk/objects/90a06f70-880a-4b5b-bd30-798710afff11/surfaces/4ba9c9f2-b768-4735-b347-28f7d576d492/">folio 20, verso</a>, column 1&#59; republished at <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oxford" class="extiw" title="w:Oxford">Oxford</a>&#58; <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bodleian_Library" class="extiw" title="w:Bodleian Library">Digital Bodleian</a>, 10 January 2019:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="enm">Forͬ fra þatt hıre <b>make</b> ıſſ dæd⹎ Ne kepeþþ ᵹho nan oþerr. Acc ſerrᵹͪeþþ aᵹᵹ forͬþı ꝥ ᵹho. Ne maᵹᫌ himͫ noƿƿhar fındenn</span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">Because whenever her <b>mate</b> is dead, she doesn't get another, but always grieves since she can't find him anywhere.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li>
<li>A <a href="/wiki/lover" title="lover">lover</a>; a <a href="/wiki/sexual" title="sexual">sexual</a> partner.</li>
<li>An <a href="/wiki/equal" title="equal">equal</a> or <a href="/wiki/match" title="match">match</a>.</li>
<li>A <a href="/wiki/comrade" title="comrade">comrade</a> or <a href="/wiki/companion" title="companion">companion</a>.</li>
<li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#rare" title="Appendix:Glossary">rare</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> A <a href="/wiki/competitor" title="competitor">competitor</a> or <a href="/wiki/opponent" title="opponent">opponent</a>.</li></ol>
