<div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Verb_5">Verb</h4><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=55" title="Edit section: Verb"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="enm">make</strong></span> 
</p>
<ol><li><span class="form-of-definition use-with-mention">alternative form of <span class="form-of-definition-link"><i class="Latn mention" lang="enm"><a href="/wiki/maken#Middle_English" title="maken">maken</a></i></span></span>
<ul><li><b>late 14th century</b>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Geoffrey_Chaucer" class="extiw" title="w:Geoffrey Chaucer">Geoffrey Chaucer</a>, The Knight's Tale, <i><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://www.gutenberg.org/files/22120/22120-h/22120-h.htm">The Canterbury Tales</a></i>, line 1053-1054:
<dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation" lang="enm">&#91;...&#93; <i>She gadereth floures, party whyte and rede,<br />To <b>make</b> a sotil gerland for hir hede,</i> &#91;...&#93;</span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">[...] She gathers flowers, mixed white and red,<br />To <b>make</b> an intricate garland for her head, [...]</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Norwegian_Bokmål"><span id="Norwegian_Bokm.C3.A5l"></span>Norwegian Bokmål</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=60" title="Edit section: Norwegian Bokmål"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_7">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=61" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Norse" class="extiw" title="w:Old Norse">Old Norse</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="non"><a href="/wiki/maki#Old_Norse" title="maki">maki</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_8">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=62" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="nb">make</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr></span> (<i>definite singular</i> <b class="Latn" lang="nb"><a href="/wiki/maken#Norwegian_Bokmål" title="maken">maken</a></b>, <i>indefinite plural</i> <b class="Latn" lang="nb"><a href="/wiki/maker#Norwegian_Bokmål" title="maker">maker</a></b>, <i>definite plural</i> <b class="Latn" lang="nb"><a href="/wiki/makene#Norwegian_Bokmål" title="makene">makene</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li>a <a href="/wiki/mate" title="mate">mate</a> <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content">especially animals and birds</span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span>, a <a href="/wiki/spouse" title="spouse">spouse</a></li>
<li>an <a href="/wiki/equal" title="equal">equal</a>, <a href="/wiki/match" title="match">match</a>, <a href="/wiki/peer" title="peer">peer</a></li>
<li>one of a pair <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content">e.g. shoe, sock</span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span></li>
<li>something that is similar or alike</li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Norwegian_Nynorsk">Norwegian Nynorsk</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=65" title="Edit section: Norwegian Nynorsk"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_8">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=66" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Norse" class="extiw" title="w:Old Norse">Old Norse</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="non"><a href="/wiki/maki#Old_Norse" title="maki">maki</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_9">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=67" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="nn">make</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr></span> (<i>definite singular</i> <b class="Latn" lang="nn"><a href="/wiki/maken#Norwegian_Nynorsk" title="maken">maken</a></b>, <i>indefinite plural</i> <b class="Latn" lang="nn"><a href="/wiki/makar#Norwegian_Nynorsk" title="makar">makar</a></b>, <i>definite plural</i> <b class="Latn" lang="nn"><a href="/wiki/makane#Norwegian_Nynorsk" title="makane">makane</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li>a <a href="/wiki/mate" title="mate">mate</a> <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content">especially animals and birds</span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span>, a <a href="/wiki/spouse" title="spouse">spouse</a></li>
<li>an <a href="/wiki/equal" title="equal">equal</a>, <a href="/wiki/match" title="match">match</a>, <a href="/wiki/peer" title="peer">peer</a></li>
<li>one of a pair <span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content qualifier-content">e.g. shoe, sock</span><span class="ib-brac qualifier-brac">)</span></li>
<li>something that is similar or alike</li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_10">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=73" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="pt">make</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="masculine gender">m</abbr> or <abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span> (<i>plural</i> <b class="Latn form-of lang-pt p-form-of" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/makes#Portuguese" title="makes">makes</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brazilian_Portuguese" class="extiw" title="w:Brazilian Portuguese">Brazil</a><span class="ib-comma label-comma">,</span>&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#informal" title="Appendix:Glossary">informal</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> <a href="/wiki/makeup" title="makeup">makeup</a> <span class="mention-gloss-paren">(</span><span class="mention-gloss">cosmetics and colorants applied to the skin</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren">)</span>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonyms:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/maquilhagem#Portuguese" title="maquilhagem">maquilhagem</a></span>, <span class="Latn" lang="pt"><a href="/wiki/maquiagem#Portuguese" title="maquiagem">maquiagem</a></span></span></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><b>2023</b> April 1, Gisela Casimiro, <cite>Estendais</cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://books.google.com.br/books?id=dm26EAAAQBAJ&amp;pg=PT96&amp;hl=en">[16]</a></sup>, Leya, <small><a href="/wiki/Special:BookSources/9789722132121" title="Special:BookSources/9789722132121">→ISBN</a></small>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Latn e-quotation cited-passage" lang="pt"><span class="q-hellip-sp"></span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#91;</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">&#93;</span><span class="q-hellip-b"> </span> nunca faço uma <i><b>make</b></i> completa, escolho sempre as coisas mais básicas, e só tive uma embalagem de base na vida.</span><dl><dd><span class="e-translation"><span class="q-hellip-sp">&#32;</span><span class="q-hellip-b">[</span>…<span class="q-hellip-b">]</span><span class="q-hellip-b">&#32;</span> I never do a full face of <b>makeup</b>, I always choose the most basic things, and I've only had one bottle of foundation in my life.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_10">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=78" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Swedish" class="extiw" title="w:Old Swedish">Old Swedish</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gmq-osw"><a href="/wiki/maki#Old_Swedish" title="maki">maki</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Norse" class="extiw" title="w:Old Norse">Old Norse</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="non"><a href="/wiki/maki#Old_Norse" title="maki">maki</a></i>, from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Germanic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Germanic language">Proto-Germanic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="gem-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/mak%C3%B4" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Germanic/makô">&#42;makô</a></i>. Doublet of <i class="Latn mention" lang="sv"><a href="/wiki/maka#Swedish" title="maka">maka</a></i>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_9">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=79" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:International_Phonetic_Alphabet" title="Wiktionary:International Phonetic Alphabet">IPA</a><sup>(<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Swedish_pronunciation" title="Appendix:Swedish pronunciation">key</a>)</sup>:&#32;<span class="IPA nowrap">/ˈmɑːˌkɛ/</span></li>
<li><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r50165410" /><table class="audiotable" style="vertical-align: middle; display: inline-block; list-style: none; line-height: 1em; border-collapse: collapse; margin: 0;"><tbody><tr><td>Audio<span class="ib-colon qualifier-colon">:</span></td><td class="audiofile"><span typeof="mw:File"><span><audio id="mwe_player_3" controls="" preload="none" data-mw-tmh="" class="mw-file-element" width="175" style="width:175px;" data-durationhint="2" data-mwtitle="Sv-make.ogg" data-mwprovider="wikimediacommons"><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/2/26/Sv-make.ogg" type="audio/ogg; codecs=&quot;vorbis&quot;" data-width="0" data-height="0" /><source src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/transcoded/2/26/Sv-make.ogg/Sv-make.ogg.mp3" type="audio/mpeg" data-transcodekey="mp3" data-width="0" data-height="0" /></audio></span></span></td><td class="audiometa" style="font-size: 80%;">(<a href="/wiki/File:Sv-make.ogg" title="File:Sv-make.ogg">file</a>)</td></tr></tbody></table></li></ul>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_12">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=make&amp;action=edit&amp;section=80" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Latn headword" lang="sv">make</strong>&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="common gender">c</abbr></span></span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="usage-label-sense"><span class="ib-brac label-brac">(</span><span class="ib-content label-content">slightly&#32;archaistic&#32;or&#32;<a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#formal" title="Appendix:Glossary">formal</a></span><span class="ib-brac label-brac">)</span></span> a <a href="/wiki/spouse" title="spouse">spouse</a>, a <a href="/wiki/husband" title="husband">husband</a>, a married <a href="/wiki/man" title="man">man</a> (mostly referring to a specific relation)
<dl><dd><span class="nyms synonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Synonym:</span> <span class="Latn" lang="sv"><a href="/wiki/man#Swedish" title="man">man</a></span></span></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="sv">Hon hade inte sett sin <b>make</b> på hela dagen.</i><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">She had not seen her <b>husband</b> all day.</span></dd></dl></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="sv"><b>Makarna</b> hade råkat ta in på samma hotell.</i><dl><dd><span class="e-translation"><b>The man and his wife</b> happened to board at the same hotel.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></li>
<li>something alike, <a href="/wiki/likes" title="likes">likes</a>
<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="sv">Restaurangen serverade sillrätter jag aldrig sett <b>maken till</b></i><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">The restaurant served herring dishes I've never seen <b>the likes of</b></span></dd></dl></div></dd>
<dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Latn mention e-example" lang="sv">Ingen hade sett svärdets <b>make</b></i><dl><dd><span class="e-translation">Nobody had seen a sword <b>like</b> this</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></li></ol>
